      <div class="gta-spotlight--opener">
      <h2 class="gta-spotlight--opener--title">Spotlight</h2>
    </div>
  
  <div class="gta-spotlight--inner">

                          <p class="gta-spotlight--eyebrow">Iran</p>
                          <h2 class="gta-spotlight--title">War with Iran</h2>
                          <p class="gta-spotlight--excerpt">Analysis from Atlantic Council experts in Washington, the Middle East, and around the world on the latest developments in the US-Israeli campaign and what’s to come for Iran and the region. </p>

<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="h-atlantic-council-global-citizen-awards"><a href="https://www.atlanticcouncil.org/global-citizen-awards/">Atlantic Council <strong>Global Citizen Awards</strong></a></h3>



<p><strong>September 23, 2026</strong></p>



<p>Held on the margins of the United Nations General Assembly in New York, the 2026 Atlantic Council <a href="https://www.atlanticcouncil.org/global-citizen-awards/">Global Citizen Awards</a> underscores our critical mission to shape the global future together, while recognizing the accomplishments of key global citizens seeking to improve the state of the world.</p>

<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="h-trump-tariff-tracker"><strong><a href="https://www.atlanticcouncil.org/programs/geoeconomics-center/trump-tariff-tracker/">Trump Tariff Tracker</a></strong></h3>



<p>The second Trump administration has embarked on a novel and aggressive tariff policy to address a range of economic and national security concerns. This tracker monitors the evolution of these tariffs and provides expert context on the economic conditions driving their creation—along with their real-world impact.</p>

	</div>

<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="h-2025-2026-annual-report"><a href="https://www.atlanticcouncil.org/in-depth-research-reports/report/2025-annual-report/">2025/2026 Annual Report</a></h3>



<p>Through the work it publishes, the ideas it generates, the future leaders it develops, and the communities it builds, the Atlantic Council shapes policy choices and strategies to create a more free, secure, and prosperous world.</p>

  <script>
    (function() {

      // email form loaded
      // (4) display form
      function hubspotFormLoadCallback(inst) {
        // console.info('fn: hubspotFormLoadCallback %o', inst);
        const form_ele = document.getElementById(`hbspt-form-${inst.instanceId}`);
        const form_ele_wrapper = document.getElementById('globalCTAForm');
        if ( form_ele && form_ele_wrapper ) {
          const relocate_form_ele = form_ele.parentElement.removeChild(form_ele);
          form_ele_wrapper.append(relocate_form_ele);
        }
      }

      // hbspt loaded
      // (3) load actual email form
      function hubspotFormLoad() {
        // console.info('fn: hubspotFormLoad', hbspt);
        if ( hbspt && hbspt.forms && typeof hbspt.forms.create === 'function' ) {
          const hbsptInstance = hbspt.forms.create({
            portalId: '43957839',
            formId: 'c93256d8-58e1-4a9f-ac49-d40f2065653e',
            region: 'na1'
          });

          if ( hbsptInstance ) {
            // console.info('o: hbsptInstance', hbsptInstance);
            hbsptInstance.onFormReady((e) => {
              // console.log('e: onFormReady', e);
              hubspotFormLoadCallback(hbsptInstance);
            });
          }
        }
      }

      // (2) track hbspt load progress
      function hubspotDeferredLoadReport() {
        // console.info('fn: hubspotDeferredLoadReport');
        if ( typeof hbspt !== 'object' ) {
          document.addEventListener('load', hubspotDeferredLoadReport, true);
        } else if ( ! hubspotDeferredLoadReport.done ) {
          document.removeEventListener('load', hubspotDeferredLoadReport, true);
          hubspotDeferredLoadReport.done = true;
          hubspotFormLoad();
        }
      }

      // (1)
      hubspotDeferredLoadReport();
    })();
  </script>

<script type="text/javascript" id="ultimate-ada-translator-js-before">
/* <![CDATA[ */
window.ultimateAdaTranslatorConfig = {"ajaxUrl":"https:\/\/www.atlanticcouncil.org\/wp-admin\/admin-ajax.php","nonce":"fa210bff1b","sourceLanguage":"en","heartbeatMs":30000,"sessionIdleMs":1800000};
//# sourceURL=ultimate-ada-translator-js-before
/* ]]> */

<script type="text/javascript" id="ultimate-ada-translator-js-after">
/* <![CDATA[ */
(function () {
	'use strict';

	var rootSelector = '[data-ultimate-ada-translator]';
	var readyAttribute = 'data-ultimate-ada-translator-ready';
	var analyticsConfig = window.ultimateAdaTranslatorConfig || null;
	var analyticsStorageKey = 'ultimateAdaTranslatorSession';
	var accessibilityStorageKey = 'ultimateAdaTranslatorAccessibilityState';
	var pageLoadedAt = Date.now();
	var lastDurationSentMs = 0;
	var didTrackTranslatorView = false;
	var documentLanguageState = null;
	var heartbeatTimer = null;
	var accessibilityDefaults = {
		zoom: 0,
		contrast: false,
		links: false,
		motion: false,
		dyslexia: false
	};
	var accessibilityZoomScales = {
		0: 1,
		1: 1.12,
		2: 1.24
	};

	function canTrackAnalytics() {
		return Boolean(
			analyticsConfig &&
			analyticsConfig.ajaxUrl &&
			analyticsConfig.nonce &&
			window.JSON &&
			window.fetch &&
			window.URLSearchParams
		);
	}

	function createSessionId() {
		var values;

		if (window.crypto && typeof window.crypto.getRandomValues === 'function') {
			values = new Uint32Array(4);
			window.crypto.getRandomValues(values);

			return Array.prototype.map.call(values, function (value) {
				return value.toString(16);
			}).join('');
		}

		return String(Date.now()) + String(Math.random()).replace(/\D/g, '');
	}

	function readSessionContext() {
		var stored;

		try {
			stored = window.sessionStorage.getItem(analyticsStorageKey);
		} catch (error) {
			stored = null;
		}

		if (!stored) {
			return null;
		}

		try {
			return JSON.parse(stored);
		} catch (error) {
			return null;
		}
	}

	function persistSessionContext(context) {
		try {
			window.sessionStorage.setItem(analyticsStorageKey, JSON.stringify(context));
		} catch (error) {
			return;
		}
	}

	function getSessionContext() {
		var context = readSessionContext();
		var now = Date.now();
		var sessionIdleMs = parseInt(analyticsConfig.sessionIdleMs || 1800000, 10);

		if (!context || !context.id || !context.lastActivityAt || now - context.lastActivityAt > sessionIdleMs) {
			context = {
				id: createSessionId(),
				startedAt: now,
				lastActivityAt: now
			};
		}

		context.lastActivityAt = now;
		persistSessionContext(context);

		return context;
	}

	function getPagePath() {
		return window.location.pathname + window.location.search;
	}

	function buildEvent(type, name, label, meta) {
		var eventMeta = {
			client_timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
			client_timezone: '',
			client_timezone_offset: new Date().getTimezoneOffset(),
			source_language: analyticsConfig && analyticsConfig.sourceLanguage ? analyticsConfig.sourceLanguage : '',
			path: getPagePath(),
			title: document.title || ''
		};
		var key;

		if (
			window.Intl &&
			window.Intl.DateTimeFormat &&
			typeof window.Intl.DateTimeFormat === 'function'
		) {
			try {
				eventMeta.client_timezone = window.Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '';
			} catch (error) {
				eventMeta.client_timezone = '';
			}
		}

		meta = meta || {};

		for (key in meta) {
			if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(meta, key)) {
				eventMeta[key] = meta[key];
			}
		}

		return {
			type: type,
			name: name || '',
			label: label || '',
			meta: eventMeta
		};
	}

	function sendAnalyticsEvents(events, useBeacon) {
		var context;
		var params;
		var beaconPayload;

		if (!canTrackAnalytics() || !events || !events.length) {
			return;
		}

		context = getSessionContext();
		params = new URLSearchParams();
		params.set('action', 'ultimate_ada_translator_track');
		params.set('nonce', analyticsConfig.nonce);
		params.set('session_id', context.id);
		params.set('events', JSON.stringify(events));

		if (useBeacon && window.navigator && typeof window.navigator.sendBeacon === 'function') {
			beaconPayload = new Blob(
				[params.toString()],
				{
					type: 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8'
				}
			);

			if (window.navigator.sendBeacon(analyticsConfig.ajaxUrl, beaconPayload)) {
				return;
			}
		}

		window.fetch(
			analyticsConfig.ajaxUrl,
			{
				method: 'POST',
				credentials: 'same-origin',
				keepalive: Boolean(useBeacon),
				headers: {
					'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8'
				},
				body: params.toString()
			}
		).catch(function () {});
	}

	function trackAnalyticsEvent(type, name, label, meta, useBeacon) {
		sendAnalyticsEvents([buildEvent(type, name, label, meta)], useBeacon);
	}

	function getDurationDelta() {
		var elapsedMs = Math.max(Date.now() - pageLoadedAt, 0);
		var deltaMs = Math.max(elapsedMs - lastDurationSentMs, 0);

		lastDurationSentMs = elapsedMs;

		return deltaMs;
	}

	function trackDurationEvent(type, useBeacon) {
		var durationMs;

		if (!didTrackTranslatorView) {
			return;
		}

		durationMs = getDurationDelta();

		if (durationMs < 1000) {
			return;
		}

		trackAnalyticsEvent(
			type,
			'duration',
			'Session Duration',
			{
				duration_ms: durationMs
			},
			useBeacon
		);
	}

	function startHeartbeat() {
		var heartbeatMs;

		if (heartbeatTimer || !canTrackAnalytics()) {
			return;
		}

		heartbeatMs = parseInt(analyticsConfig.heartbeatMs || 30000, 10);
		heartbeatTimer = window.setInterval(function () {
			if (document.visibilityState && document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
				return;
			}

			trackDurationEvent('duration_ping', false);
		}, heartbeatMs);
	}

	function trackTranslatorViewOnce() {
		if (didTrackTranslatorView) {
			return;
		}

		didTrackTranslatorView = true;
		trackAnalyticsEvent('translator_view', 'view', 'Translator View', {}, false);
		startHeartbeat();
	}

	function normalizeValue(value) {
		var normalized = String(value || '').toLowerCase();

		if (typeof normalized.normalize === 'function') {
			normalized = normalized.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '');
		}

		return normalized;
	}

	function getLanguages(select) {
		return Array.prototype.map.call(select.options, function (option) {
			return {
				value: option.value,
				googleCode: option.getAttribute('data-google-code') || option.value,
				direction: option.getAttribute('data-direction') || 'ltr',
				label: option.textContent || option.innerText || '',
				search: option.getAttribute('data-search') || ''
			};
		});
	}

	function findLanguage(languages, value) {
		var index;

		for (index = 0; index < languages.length; index += 1) {
			if (languages[index].value === value) {
				return languages[index];
			}
		}

		return null;
	}

	function findLanguageByGoogleCode(languages, googleCode) {
		var index;

		for (index = 0; index < languages.length; index += 1) {
			if (languages[index].googleCode === googleCode) {
				return languages[index];
			}
		}

		return null;
	}

	function getMatches(languages, query) {
		var normalizedQuery = normalizeValue(query);

		return languages.filter(function (language) {
			var searchable = normalizeValue(language.value + ' ' + language.googleCode + ' ' + language.label + ' ' + language.search);

			return !normalizedQuery || searchable.indexOf(normalizedQuery) !== -1;
		});
	}

	function clearNode(node) {
		while (node && node.firstChild) {
			node.removeChild(node.firstChild);
		}
	}

	function cloneAccessibilityDefaults() {
		return {
			zoom: accessibilityDefaults.zoom,
			contrast: accessibilityDefaults.contrast,
			links: accessibilityDefaults.links,
			motion: accessibilityDefaults.motion,
			dyslexia: accessibilityDefaults.dyslexia
		};
	}

	function normalizeAccessibilityState(state) {
		var normalized = cloneAccessibilityDefaults();

		if (!state) {
			return normalized;
		}

		normalized.zoom = parseInt(state.zoom, 10);

		if (isNaN(normalized.zoom) || normalized.zoom < 0) {
			normalized.zoom = 0;
		} else if (normalized.zoom > 2) {
			normalized.zoom = 2;
		}

		normalized.contrast = Boolean(state.contrast);
		normalized.links = Boolean(state.links);
		normalized.motion = Boolean(state.motion);
		normalized.dyslexia = Boolean(state.dyslexia);

		return normalized;
	}

	function isAccessibilityStateActive(state) {
		var normalized = normalizeAccessibilityState(state);

		return normalized.zoom > 0 || normalized.contrast || normalized.links || normalized.motion || normalized.dyslexia;
	}

	function getTextZoomTargets() {
		if (!document.body) {
			return [];
		}

		return Array.prototype.filter.call(document.body.querySelectorAll('*'), function (element) {
			var tag = element && element.tagName ? element.tagName.toLowerCase() : '';

			if (!tag) {
				return false;
			}

			return [
				'script', 'style', 'noscript', 'svg', 'path', 'circle', 'rect',
				'polygon', 'line', 'iframe', 'canvas', 'video', 'audio', 'img',
				'picture', 'source', 'br', 'hr'
			].indexOf(tag) === -1;
		});
	}

	function rememberOriginalTextMetrics() {
		getTextZoomTargets().forEach(function (element) {
			var computed;
			var fontSize;
			var lineHeight;

			if (element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalFontSize && element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalLineHeightCaptured === 'true') {
				return;
			}

			computed = window.getComputedStyle(element);

			if (!element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalFontSize) {
				fontSize = parseFloat(computed.fontSize);

				if (!isNaN(fontSize) && fontSize > 0) {
					element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalFontSize = String(fontSize);
				}
			}

			if (element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalLineHeightCaptured !== 'true') {
				lineHeight = null;

				if (computed.lineHeight && computed.lineHeight.slice(-2) === 'px') {
					lineHeight = parseFloat(computed.lineHeight);

					if (isNaN(lineHeight) || lineHeight <= 0) {
						lineHeight = null;
					}
				}

				if (lineHeight !== null) {
					element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalLineHeight = String(lineHeight);
				}

				element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalLineHeightCaptured = 'true';
			}
		});
	}

	function applyTextZoom(level) {
		var scale = accessibilityZoomScales[level] || 1;

		rememberOriginalTextMetrics();

		getTextZoomTargets().forEach(function (element) {
			var originalFontSize = parseFloat(element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalFontSize || '');
			var originalLineHeight = parseFloat(element.dataset.ultimateAdaOriginalLineHeight || '');

			if (!isNaN(originalFontSize) && originalFontSize > 0) {
				element.style.fontSize = (originalFontSize * scale).toFixed(2) + 'px';
			}

			if (!isNaN(originalLineHeight) && originalLineHeight > 0) {
				element.style.lineHeight = (originalLineHeight * scale).toFixed(2) + 'px';
			}
		});
	}

	function readAccessibilityState() {
		var body = document.body;

		if (!body) {
			return cloneAccessibilityDefaults();
		}

		return normalizeAccessibilityState({
			zoom: parseInt(body.getAttribute('data-ultimate-ada-access-zoom') || '0', 10) || 0,
			contrast: body.classList.contains('ultimate-ada-translator-access-contrast'),
			links: body.classList.contains('ultimate-ada-translator-access-links'),
			motion: body.classList.contains('ultimate-ada-translator-access-motion'),
			dyslexia: body.classList.contains('ultimate-ada-translator-access-dyslexia')
		});
	}

	function saveAccessibilityState() {
		try {
			window.localStorage.setItem(accessibilityStorageKey, JSON.stringify(readAccessibilityState()));
		} catch (error) {}
	}

	function applyAccessibilityState(state) {
		var body = document.body;
		var normalized = normalizeAccessibilityState(state);

		if (!body) {
			return;
		}

		body.classList.toggle('ultimate-ada-translator-access-contrast', normalized.contrast);
		body.classList.toggle('ultimate-ada-translator-access-links', normalized.links);
		body.classList.toggle('ultimate-ada-translator-access-motion', normalized.motion);
		body.classList.toggle('ultimate-ada-translator-access-dyslexia', normalized.dyslexia);
		body.setAttribute('data-ultimate-ada-access-zoom', String(normalized.zoom));

		applyTextZoom(normalized.zoom);
	}

	function loadAccessibilityState() {
		var parsedState = cloneAccessibilityDefaults();
		var storedState;

		try {
			storedState = window.localStorage.getItem(accessibilityStorageKey);

			if (storedState) {
				parsedState = JSON.parse(storedState);
			}
		} catch (error) {
			parsedState = cloneAccessibilityDefaults();
		}

		applyAccessibilityState(parsedState);
	}

	function getAccessibilityStatusText(state) {
		var normalized = normalizeAccessibilityState(state);
		var activeSettings = [];

		if (normalized.zoom === 1) {
			activeSettings.push('medium text size');
		} else if (normalized.zoom === 2) {
			activeSettings.push('large text size');
		}

		if (normalized.contrast) {
			activeSettings.push('high contrast');
		}

		if (normalized.links) {
			activeSettings.push('highlight links');
		}

		if (normalized.motion) {
			activeSettings.push('reduced motion');
		}

		if (normalized.dyslexia) {
			activeSettings.push('dyslexia-friendly font');
		}

		if (!activeSettings.length) {
			return 'Accessibility tools are set to default.';
		}

		return 'Active accessibility settings: ' + activeSettings.join(', ') + '.';
	}

	function syncAccessibilityButtons(scope) {
		var root = scope || document;
		var state = readAccessibilityState();
		var zoomLabel = 'Text Size: Default';

		if (state.zoom === 1) {
			zoomLabel = 'Text Size: Medium';
		} else if (state.zoom === 2) {
			zoomLabel = 'Text Size: Large';
		}

		Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-accessibility="zoom"]'), function (button) {
			button.textContent = zoomLabel;
			button.classList.toggle('is-active', state.zoom > 0);
			button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', state.zoom > 0 ? 'true' : 'false');
		});

		['contrast', 'links', 'motion', 'dyslexia'].forEach(function (control) {
			Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-accessibility="' + control + '"]'), function (button) {
				var isActive = Boolean(state[control]);

				button.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
				button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', isActive ? 'true' : 'false');
			});
		});

		Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-accessibility-status]'), function (statusNode) {
			statusNode.textContent = getAccessibilityStatusText(state);
		});
	}

	function updateAccessibilityState(control) {
		var state = readAccessibilityState();

		if (control === 'zoom') {
			state.zoom = state.zoom >= 2 ? 0 : state.zoom + 1;
		} else if (control === 'contrast' || control === 'links' || control === 'motion' || control === 'dyslexia') {
			state[control] = !state[control];
		} else if (control === 'reset') {
			state = cloneAccessibilityDefaults();
		}

		applyAccessibilityState(state);
		saveAccessibilityState();
		syncAccessibilityButtons(document);
	}

	function createLanguageButton(language, selectedValue) {
		var button = document.createElement('button');
		var label = document.createElement('span');
		var code = document.createElement('span');

		button.type = 'button';
		button.className = 'ultimate-ada-translator__language-button';
		button.setAttribute('data-ultimate-ada-translator-language', language.value);
		button.setAttribute('data-google-code', language.googleCode);
		button.setAttribute('dir', language.direction);
		button.setAttribute('lang', language.value);
		button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', language.value === selectedValue ? 'true' : 'false');
		button.classList.toggle('is-active', language.value === selectedValue);

		label.textContent = language.label;
		code.className = 'ultimate-ada-translator__language-code';
		code.textContent = language.value;

		button.appendChild(label);
		button.appendChild(code);

		return button;
	}

	function renderOptions(list, empty, languages, query, selectedValue) {
		var matches = getMatches(languages, query);

		clearNode(list);

		if (!matches.length) {
			if (empty) {
				empty.hidden = false;
			}
			return;
		}

		if (empty) {
			empty.hidden = true;
		}

		matches.forEach(function (language) {
			list.appendChild(createLanguageButton(language, selectedValue));
		});
	}

	function updateStatus(root, status, languages, selectedValue) {
		var selectedLanguage = findLanguage(languages, selectedValue);

		root.setAttribute('data-selected-language', selectedValue);

		if (!status || !selectedLanguage) {
			return;
		}

		status.textContent = 'Current language: ' + selectedLanguage.label;
	}

	function captureDocumentLanguageState() {
		var html = document.documentElement;

		if (documentLanguageState || !html) {
			return;
		}

		documentLanguageState = {
			hasDirection: html.hasAttribute('dir'),
			direction: html.getAttribute('dir'),
			hasLanguage: html.hasAttribute('lang'),
			language: html.getAttribute('lang')
		};
	}

	function restoreDocumentLanguageState() {
		var html = document.documentElement;
		var body = document.body;

		captureDocumentLanguageState();

		if (!html || !documentLanguageState) {
			return;
		}

		if (documentLanguageState.hasDirection) {
			html.setAttribute('dir', documentLanguageState.direction || 'ltr');
		} else {
			html.removeAttribute('dir');
		}

		if (documentLanguageState.hasLanguage) {
			html.setAttribute('lang', documentLanguageState.language || 'en');
		} else {
			html.removeAttribute('lang');
		}

		html.classList.remove('ultimate-ada-translator-rtl', 'ultimate-ada-translator-ltr');

		if (body) {
			body.classList.remove('ultimate-ada-translator-rtl', 'ultimate-ada-translator-ltr');
		}
	}

	function applyDocumentLanguageState(language) {
		var html = document.documentElement;
		var body = document.body;
		var direction = language && language.direction === 'rtl' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
		var languageCode = language && (language.googleCode || language.value);

		captureDocumentLanguageState();

		if (!html || !languageCode) {
			return;
		}

		html.setAttribute('dir', direction);
		html.setAttribute('lang', languageCode);
		html.classList.toggle('ultimate-ada-translator-rtl', direction === 'rtl');
		html.classList.toggle('ultimate-ada-translator-ltr', direction !== 'rtl');

		if (body) {
			body.classList.toggle('ultimate-ada-translator-rtl', direction === 'rtl');
			body.classList.toggle('ultimate-ada-translator-ltr', direction !== 'rtl');
		}
	}

	function dispatchLanguageChange(root, selectedValue) {
		var event;

		if (typeof window.CustomEvent !== 'function') {
			return;
		}

		event = new window.CustomEvent(
			'ultimateAdaTranslator:languageChange',
			{
				bubbles: true,
				detail: {
					language: selectedValue
				}
			}
		);

		root.dispatchEvent(event);
	}

	function getActiveTab(root) {
		return root.getAttribute('data-active-tab') || 'language';
	}

	function syncTranslatorTabs(root) {
		var activeTab = getActiveTab(root);

		Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-tab]'), function (button) {
			var isActive = button.getAttribute('data-ultimate-ada-translator-tab') === activeTab;

			button.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
			button.setAttribute('aria-selected', isActive ? 'true' : 'false');
			button.tabIndex = isActive ? 0 : -1;
		});

		Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-tab-panel]'), function (panel) {
			var isActive = panel.getAttribute('data-ultimate-ada-translator-tab-panel') === activeTab;

			panel.hidden = !isActive;
			panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', isActive ? 'false' : 'true');
		});
	}

	function setActiveTab(root, tabName) {
		root.setAttribute('data-active-tab', tabName === 'accessibility' ? 'accessibility' : 'language');
		syncTranslatorTabs(root);
	}

	function focusActiveTabControl(root) {
		var activeTab = getActiveTab(root);
		var search;
		var firstAccessibilityButton;

		if (activeTab === 'accessibility') {
			firstAccessibilityButton = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-accessibility]');

			if (firstAccessibilityButton) {
				firstAccessibilityButton.focus();
			}

			return;
		}

		search = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-search]');

		if (search) {
			search.focus();
		}
	}

	function setPanelState(root, isOpen) {
		var trigger = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-trigger]');
		var panel = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-panel]');
		var backdrop = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-backdrop]');

		if (isOpen) {
			closeAllTranslators(root);
		}

		root.setAttribute('data-open', isOpen ? 'true' : 'false');

		if (trigger) {
			trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
		}

		if (panel) {
			panel.hidden = !isOpen;
		}

		if (backdrop) {
			backdrop.hidden = !isOpen;
		}

		if (isOpen) {
			syncTranslatorTabs(root);
		}

		updateLightboxBodyState();
	}

	function closeAllTranslators(exceptRoot) {
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll(rootSelector + '[data-open="true"]'), function (root) {
			if (exceptRoot && root === exceptRoot) {
				return;
			}

			root.setAttribute('data-open', 'false');

			var trigger = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-trigger]');
			var panel = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-panel]');
			var backdrop = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-backdrop]');

			if (trigger) {
				trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
			}

			if (panel) {
				panel.hidden = true;
			}

			if (backdrop) {
				backdrop.hidden = true;
			}
		});
	}

	function updateLightboxBodyState() {
		if (!document.body) {
			return;
		}

		document.body.classList.toggle(
			'ultimate-ada-translator-lightbox-open',
			Boolean(document.querySelector(rootSelector + '[data-open="true"]'))
		);
	}

	function trackSelectedLanguage(languages, selectedValue) {
		var selectedLanguage = findLanguage(languages, selectedValue);

		if (!selectedLanguage) {
			return;
		}

		trackAnalyticsEvent(
			'language_select',
			selectedLanguage.value,
			selectedLanguage.label,
			{
				language_code: selectedLanguage.value
			},
			false
		);
	}

	function getCookieDomains(hostname) {
		var normalizedHost = String(hostname || '').replace(/^\.+/, '');
		var parts = normalizedHost.split('.').filter(Boolean);
		var domains = ['', normalizedHost, '.' + normalizedHost];
		var index;
		var domain;

		for (index = 1; index < parts.length - 1; index += 1) {
			domain = parts.slice(index).join('.');
			domains.push(domain, '.' + domain);
		}

		return domains.filter(function (value, valueIndex) {
			return value && domains.indexOf(value) === valueIndex;
		});
	}

	function writeCookie(name, value, domain) {
		var domainPart = domain ? '; domain=' + domain : '';

		document.cookie = name + '=' + encodeURIComponent(value) + '; path=/' + domainPart + '; SameSite=Lax';
	}

	function expireCookie(name, domain) {
		var domainPart = domain ? '; domain=' + domain : '';

		document.cookie = name + '=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:01 GMT; Max-Age=0; path=/' + domainPart + '; SameSite=Lax';
	}

	function persistGoogleTranslateState(googleCode, sourceGoogleCode) {
		var cookieValue = '/' + (sourceGoogleCode || 'en') + '/' + googleCode;

		getCookieDomains(window.location.hostname).forEach(function (domain) {
			writeCookie('googtrans', cookieValue, domain);
		});

		writeCookie('googtrans', cookieValue, '');

		try {
			window.localStorage.setItem('googtrans', cookieValue);
			window.sessionStorage.setItem('googtrans', cookieValue);
		} catch (error) {}
	}

	function clearGoogleTranslateState() {
		getCookieDomains(window.location.hostname).forEach(function (domain) {
			expireCookie('googtrans', domain);
		});

		expireCookie('googtrans', '');

		try {
			window.localStorage.removeItem('googtrans');
			window.sessionStorage.removeItem('googtrans');
		} catch (error) {}

		document.documentElement.classList.remove('translated-ltr', 'translated-rtl');

		if (document.body) {
			document.body.classList.remove('translated-ltr', 'translated-rtl');
			document.body.style.top = '';
		}
	}

	function getTranslateSelect() {
		return document.querySelector('.goog-te-combo');
	}

	function getCurrentGoogleCode(sourceGoogleCode) {
		var select = getTranslateSelect();
		var match;
		var parts;

		if (select && select.value) {
			return select.value;
		}

		match = document.cookie.match(/(?:^|;\s*)googtrans=([^;]+)/);

		if (!match) {
			return sourceGoogleCode || 'en';
		}

		parts = decodeURIComponent(match[1]).split('/');

		return parts[2] || sourceGoogleCode || 'en';
	}

	function waitForTranslateSelect(callback, attempts) {
		var maxAttempts = 60;
		var currentAttempts = attempts || 0;
		var select = getTranslateSelect();

		if (select) {
			callback(select);
			return;
		}

		if (currentAttempts >= maxAttempts) {
			return;
		}

		window.setTimeout(function () {
			waitForTranslateSelect(callback, currentAttempts + 1);
		}, 250);
	}

	function reloadCurrentUrl() {
		window.location.assign(window.location.origin + window.location.pathname + window.location.search);
	}

	function applyWebsiteTranslation(language, root) {
		var googleCode = language.googleCode;
		var sourceValue = root.getAttribute('data-source-language') || root.getAttribute('data-default-language') || 'en';
		var sourceGoogleCode = root.getAttribute('data-source-google-code') || sourceValue;

		if (language.value === sourceValue || googleCode === sourceGoogleCode) {
			restoreDocumentLanguageState();
			clearGoogleTranslateState();

			waitForTranslateSelect(function (select) {
				select.value = '';
				select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', {bubbles: true, cancelable: true}));
			});

			reloadCurrentUrl();
			return;
		}

		waitForTranslateSelect(function (select) {
			var optionExists = Array.prototype.some.call(select.options, function (option) {
				return option.value === googleCode;
			});

			persistGoogleTranslateState(googleCode, sourceGoogleCode);

			if (optionExists) {
				select.value = googleCode;
				select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', {bubbles: true, cancelable: true}));

				window.setTimeout(function () {
					if (getCurrentGoogleCode(sourceGoogleCode) === googleCode) {
						applyDocumentLanguageState(language);
					} else {
						reloadCurrentUrl();
					}
				}, 2200);
			} else {
				reloadCurrentUrl();
			}

			setPanelState(root, false);
		});
	}

	function initTranslator(root) {
		var trigger = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-trigger]');
		var panel = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-panel]');
		var backdrop = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-backdrop]');
		var closeButton = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-close]');
		var tabButtons = root.querySelectorAll('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-tab]');
		var search = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-search]');
		var select = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-select]');
		var list = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-list]');
		var empty = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-empty]');
		var reset = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-reset]');
		var status = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-status]');
		var accessibilityControls = root.querySelectorAll('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-accessibility]');
		var defaultValue = root.getAttribute('data-default-language') || 'en';
		var sourceGoogleCode = root.getAttribute('data-source-google-code') || defaultValue;
		var currentGoogleCode;
		var currentLanguage;
		var selectedValue;
		var languages;

		if (root.getAttribute(readyAttribute) === 'true' || !trigger || !panel || !backdrop || !closeButton || !search || !select || !list || !reset) {
			return;
		}

		root.setAttribute(readyAttribute, 'true');
		panel.hidden = true;
		backdrop.hidden = true;
		syncTranslatorTabs(root);
		languages = getLanguages(select);
		currentGoogleCode = getCurrentGoogleCode(sourceGoogleCode);
		currentLanguage = findLanguageByGoogleCode(languages, currentGoogleCode);
		selectedValue = currentLanguage ? currentLanguage.value : defaultValue;

		if (currentLanguage && currentLanguage.value !== defaultValue) {
			applyDocumentLanguageState(currentLanguage);
		} else {
			restoreDocumentLanguageState();
		}

		renderOptions(list, empty, languages, search.value, selectedValue);
		updateStatus(root, status, languages, selectedValue);
		syncAccessibilityButtons(root);

		trigger.addEventListener('click', function () {
			var isOpen = root.getAttribute('data-open') === 'true';

			if (!isOpen) {
				setActiveTab(root, 'language');
			}

			setPanelState(root, !isOpen);

			if (!isOpen) {
				window.setTimeout(function () {
					focusActiveTabControl(root);
				}, 0);
			}
		});

		backdrop.addEventListener('click', function () {
			setPanelState(root, false);
			trigger.focus();
		});

		closeButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
			setPanelState(root, false);
			trigger.focus();
		});

		Array.prototype.forEach.call(tabButtons, function (button) {
			button.addEventListener('click', function () {
				setActiveTab(root, button.getAttribute('data-ultimate-ada-translator-tab') || 'language');
				focusActiveTabControl(root);
			});
		});

		search.addEventListener('input', function () {
			renderOptions(list, empty, languages, search.value, selectedValue);
		});

		list.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
			var button = event.target && event.target.closest ? event.target.closest('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-language]') : null;
			var language;

			if (!button) {
				return;
			}

			event.preventDefault();
			language = findLanguage(languages, button.getAttribute('data-ultimate-ada-translator-language'));

			if (!language) {
				return;
			}

			selectedValue = language.value;
			select.value = selectedValue;
			renderOptions(list, empty, languages, search.value, selectedValue);
			updateStatus(root, status, languages, selectedValue);
			dispatchLanguageChange(root, selectedValue);
			trackSelectedLanguage(languages, selectedValue);
			if (selectedValue === defaultValue) {
				restoreDocumentLanguageState();
			}
			applyWebsiteTranslation(language, root);
		});

		reset.addEventListener('click', function () {
			selectedValue = defaultValue;
			restoreDocumentLanguageState();
			search.value = '';
			select.value = selectedValue;
			renderOptions(list, empty, languages, search.value, selectedValue);
			updateStatus(root, status, languages, selectedValue);
			dispatchLanguageChange(root, selectedValue);
			trackAnalyticsEvent(
				'reset',
				defaultValue,
				(findLanguage(languages, defaultValue) || {label: defaultValue}).label,
				{
					language_code: defaultValue
				},
				false
			);
			applyWebsiteTranslation(findLanguage(languages, defaultValue) || {value: defaultValue, googleCode: sourceGoogleCode, direction: 'ltr', label: defaultValue}, root);
		});

		Array.prototype.forEach.call(accessibilityControls, function (button) {
			button.addEventListener('click', function () {
				updateAccessibilityState(button.getAttribute('data-ultimate-ada-translator-accessibility') || '');
			});
		});
	}

	function initAllTranslators() {
		var translators = document.querySelectorAll(rootSelector);

		loadAccessibilityState();
		syncAccessibilityButtons(document);

		Array.prototype.forEach.call(translators, initTranslator);

		if (translators.length) {
			trackTranslatorViewOnce();
		}
	}

	window.addEventListener('pagehide', function () {
		trackDurationEvent('pagehide', true);
	});

	document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
		if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
			trackDurationEvent('pagehide', true);
		}
	});

	document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll(rootSelector + '[data-open="true"]'), function (root) {
			if (!root.contains(event.target)) {
				setPanelState(root, false);
			}
		});
	});

	document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
		if (event.key !== 'Escape') {
			return;
		}

		Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll(rootSelector + '[data-open="true"]'), function (root) {
			var trigger = root.querySelector('[data-ultimate-ada-translator-trigger]');

			setPanelState(root, false);

			if (trigger) {
				trigger.focus();
			}
		});
	});

	if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
		document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initAllTranslators);
	} else {
		initAllTranslators();
	}
}());
//# sourceURL=ultimate-ada-translator-js-after
/* ]]> */
</script>
